Actual Answer

Pain Management Technologies (PMT) offers several light therapy devices that use red, infrared, and multi‑spectrum LEDs for pain relief, circulation support, tissue warming, muscle relaxation, and cosmetic skin benefits.

Key light therapy products and information from PMT include:

1) NerveBeam LED Light Therapy Wrap (Clinical Device)
- Product Code: NBRT175
- Type: High‑powered red and infrared LED light therapy wrap for broad‑surface, clinical use.
- Wavelengths:
  - Red light: 660 nm – positioned for surface‑level tissue exposure.
  - Infrared light: 830 nm and 850 nm – intended for surface and deeper thermal support.
- Intended effects and benefits:
  - Elevate tissue temperature.
  - Temporarily relieve minor muscle, joint, and nerve‑related pain.
  - Temporarily improve local blood circulation in targeted treatment areas.
  - Promote muscle relaxation, reduce tension, and support range of motion.
  - Support the body’s natural healing processes on a cellular level.
- Design features:
  - High LED density and broad treatment coverage.
  - Flexible straps for freeform placement across multiple body areas.

2) The NerveBeam LED Light Therapy Wrap – Red & Infrared, 22,000 mW (Pair)
- Product Code: NBRT175 PAIR
- Type: High‑powered LED red and infrared therapy device (pair of wraps).
- Total Power: 22,000 mW.
- LED Count: 600 individual LED diodes.
- Temperature: Reaches temperatures over 100°F.
- Registration: FDA registered device.
- Wavelengths and roles:
  - Red light (660 nm):
    - Visible, acts mainly at the skin surface.
    - Considered most effective at the surface of the skin.
    - Provides an analgesic (pain‑relieving) and anti‑inflammatory response.
  - Infrared light (830 nm and 850 nm):
    - Invisible light.
    - Effective at the surface and penetrates the skin by about 1.5 inches.
    - Delivers heat into tissue for temporary pain relief.
    - Promotes relaxation of muscle tissue and helps relieve stiffness.
- Reported benefits:
  - Elevate tissue temperature.
  - Temporarily improve blood circulation.
  - Temporarily relieve minor muscle and joint pain.
  - Support muscle relaxation and comfort.

3) Multi‑Spectrum Red Light Therapy for Face (Infarex)
- Product Code: PMTLTF
- Type: Multi‑spectrum cosmetic and skin‑focused LED device for the face.
- Total LEDs: 240 total LEDs.
- Wavelengths:
  - Red and near‑infrared: 660 nm and 850 nm (noted as best combination for cosmetic therapy in this device’s description).
- Power:
  - Total power irradiance: 46 mW/cm² (clinical‑grade power level stated for the device).
- Construction & design:
  - 100% silicone (very flexible, soft, durable, and skin‑safe).
  - Portable with a rechargeable battery (can be worn while moving, exercising, etc.).
- Use guidance:
  - Recommended session time: 15–20 minutes per session.
  - Recommended frequency: 3–4 times per week.
- Seven LED light spectrums and described roles:
  - Cyan LED:
    - Enhances cell energy.
    - Described as helping metabolism and having a calming, anti‑inflammatory effect.
    - Used to help prevent acne, purify the skin, and soothe the skin surface.
  - Blue LED:
    - Described as supporting wound healing and “cleaning” the skin.
    - May enhance natural healing by increasing blood flow and stimulating collagen and fibroblasts.
  - Red LED:
    - May reduce inflammation.
    - May stimulate collagen production (associated with younger‑looking, more elastic skin).
  - Green LED:
    - Used to treat dilated capillaries, dark under‑eye circles, hyperpigmentation, sunspots, and sagging skin around the eyes.
  - Yellow LED:
    - Stimulates production of red blood cells, supporting skin healing and rejuvenation.
    - Shallow penetration, used for conditions with redness.
  - Infrared LED:
    - Reduces skin and tissue inflammation.
    - Speeds healing of lesions and wounds.
    - Stimulates collagen and elastin production for firmer, more supple, younger‑looking skin.
  - (Additional violet/purple/green spectrum references are part of the 7‑spectrum design, all targeted to cosmetic and skin‑appearance benefits.)

4) General themes from PMT’s light therapy offerings
- Modalities used:
  - Red light therapy (around 660 nm).
  - Infrared/Near‑infrared light therapy (around 830–850 nm).
  - Multi‑spectrum cosmetic LED therapy (red, blue, cyan, yellow, purple, green, violet).
- Primary purposes across products:
  - Pain‑related: Temporary relief of minor muscle, joint, and some nerve‑related pain; tissue warming; relaxation of muscles; improved local circulation.
  - Skin and cosmetic: Support collagen and elastin, reduce visible signs of aging, assist with redness, hyperpigmentation, under‑eye circles, mild acne support, and general skin rejuvenation.
- Design priorities:
  - Clinical‑grade power levels where noted.
  - Broad coverage and high LED counts for wraps (e.g., 600 LEDs in the NerveBeam pair).
  - Flexible, adjustable straps or silicone materials for comfortable, adaptable placement or wear.
  - Portable, rechargeable operation for convenience.

If you’d like, tell me whether you’re more interested in pain relief (like NerveBeam wraps) or skin/cosmetic benefits (like the facial multi‑spectrum device), and I can focus on that specific light therapy product.

Actual Answer

Premium Pneumatic Cervical Traction Collar – Inflatable Neck Traction Device for Pain Relief, Stretch and Decompress Neck Muscles

Portable device for neck pain relief. Efficiently supports neck and head, anatomically designed. Revolutionizes home cervical traction. Thin and light weight.

PRODUCT CODE:
CVT2000

THERAPY INFO / DESCRIPTION
Cervical Traction

Soft Neck Air Traction Pro is a brand new neck massager. Improved model with an easy to use air pump. Soft Neck Air Traction is comfortable and can be used anywhere.

The cervical neck traction device is an air-inflated neck-stretching device that treats most neck problems. This air neck traction device is made up of lightweight soft rubber and covered with soft textile at the top and bottom, remaining portable and convenient for users on the go.

This portable air neck traction device has several built-in air chambers and provides an effective, gentle, even traction particularly suitable for office workers and people with whiplash.

For the air traction devices, use the inflation pump to slowly raise the pressure to a comfortable stretch. The traction allows the tight neck muscles to relax and relieve the pressure in the joints and nerves.

How to use Air Neck Traction?
Simply place the air neck traction device around your neck to bring relief on the spot. The ChiSoft neck traction device can be taken anywhere to massage your neck. No assembly or batteries are required to operate the unit. The simplicity of the design makes it the ideal tool for home or office use. No longer do you have to wait until you get home to seek relief, or lie on the floor to use another product.

This air neck traction device has three inflatable layers; you can deflate the upper layer if you have a short neck length. This unique feature is only available on the pro neck traction device.

Indications:
- Tight muscles
- Pinched nerves
- Neck pain
- Tension headaches
- Osteoarthritis
- Swollen discs
- Straightening of the neck
- Degenerative discs or joints.

SPECIFICATIONS
Product Weight (lbs): 5
Width (cms): 8
Length (inches): 5
Weight Code: G

For electrotherapy (TENS) specifically, PMT offers separate TENS units that can complement traction-based approaches, but the inflatable neck traction collar itself is a pneumatic traction device, not a TENS unit.

Actual Answer

NerveWave is an Advanced Neuro-Modulating Electrotherapy System designed as a clinical, multi-domain electrotherapy platform to therapeutically treat pain, stimulate nerves, improve circulation, support regeneration, and accelerate restorative recovery across neuropathy, chronic pain, and rehabilitation care pathways.

Key purposes and outcomes:
• Pain modulation – Helps calm hypersensitive nerve signaling and reduce chronic pain patterns.
• Nerve support – Supports regeneration, neuro-protection, and improved nerve function.
• Circulatory enhancement – Promotes microcirculation, lymphatic flow, and edema reduction.
• Functional recovery – Assists muscle activation, tissue repair, and rehabilitation progress.
• Autonomic regulation – Supports parasympathetic activation for sleep, stress, and migraine management.

Core electrotherapy capabilities and modes:
• Multi-domain electrotherapy platform with advanced neuro‑modulating programming.
• Manual modes include: TENS, EMS, IFC (Interferential), MICRO (microcurrent), Russian, and Galvanic.
• Regenerative Pain Control: Monophasic High Volt and PMT Wave (Decaying Spike) for joint and back applications.
• Nerve & Neuropathy programs using:
  – Middle / High Frequency: 4000 Hz with or without a beat frequency.
  – Low Frequency: 7.83 Hz.
• Vagus Nerve Stimulation: Auricular placement (Tragus / Cymba Concha) for systemic regulation.
• Direct Current modes: Used to mimic natural bioelectric signals, support tissue repair, alter local blood flow to reduce swelling, and modulate nerve pathways.
• Electro‑Acupuncture capability: Targets specific meridian points using electrotherapy specifications studied to support regenerative processes.

Program index (example pre‑configured therapies):
• Regenerative Pain Control – Joint (60 min)
• Regenerative Pain Control – Back (25 min)
• Neuropathy – Primary Phase (20 min)
• Neuropathy – Secondary Phase (20 min)
• Neuropathy – Maintenance Phase (30 min)
• Restorative Recovery – Rehabilitative Relief (30 min)
• Restorative Recovery – Muscle Activation (30 min)
• Vagus Nerve – Sleep, Anxiety, Migraine (10 min)

Clinical focus areas:
• Neuropathy & nerve pain: Neuro‑modulating programs aimed at silencing aberrant nerve signaling while broader treatment strategies support regeneration and neuro‑protection.
• Restorative recovery & rehab: Post‑surgical or post‑injury rehabilitation, muscle activation, and neuromuscular re‑education, including cases involving scar tissue nerve entrapment and musculoskeletal sports injuries (such as tennis elbow, golfer’s elbow, plantar fasciitis, muscle tears, and sprains).
• Autonomic / vagus nerve support: Programs intended for sleep, stress/anxiety, and migraine‑related applications through auricular vagus nerve stimulation.

Overall, NerveWave integrates advanced neuro‑modulating programming with traditional clinical‑grade electrotherapy modalities in one system, focusing on pain relief, nerve function support, circulation, and restorative recovery, particularly for neuropathy and chronic pain conditions.

PRODUCT CODE: NWNSC

Actual Answer

The Cloud Walker Walking Boot is a durable, medical-grade orthopedic support device designed to help with recovery from foot, ankle, or lower leg injuries.

Key features and benefits:

• Full Shell Protection – The rigid outer shell offers maximum support and stability for foot, ankle, and lower leg injuries. It helps protect the injured area during walking and daily activities.

• Adjustable Air Cell System – Built-in air chambers allow you to personalize compression around the foot and ankle. This adjustable air system is designed to improve comfort and help reduce swelling.

• Shock-Absorbing Sole – The cushioned, shock-absorbing sole minimizes impact with each step and promotes a more natural walking motion during recovery, helping reduce stress on the injured area.

• Universal Fit – The boot is designed to fit either the left or right foot. Adjustable straps allow a secure and customized fit for different leg and foot shapes.

• Post-Operative & Injury Support – It is intended for use with fractures, sprains, soft tissue injuries, and post-surgery recovery, providing stability and protection while you heal.

• Lightweight Design – The overall construction is made to be supportive yet lightweight, helping users move more comfortably during rehabilitation.

Sizes and product codes:
• Small – PRODUCT CODE: CWBS
• Medium – PRODUCT CODE: CWBM
• Large – PRODUCT CODE: CWBL

There is also a 17" tall version (Cloud Walker Full Shell 17" Tall Walking Boot) that provides enhanced lower leg support, with:
• 17" tall protective design for added stabilization of the foot and ankle
• Adjustable hook-and-loop straps for a secure and customizable fit
• Durable solid toe box and reinforced frame to reduce unwanted movement
• Comfortable cushioned interior to help minimize pressure points during extended wear
• Non-slip rocker sole for smoother steps and improved balance on indoor surfaces
• Medium size code: CWB17M

The Cloud Walker line is intended to support both short-term and long-term rehabilitation by providing stability, protection, and adjustable comfort during the healing process. Remember, for medical decisions about when and how to use this type of boot, you should follow guidance from your healthcare provider.

Actual Answer

The Ultima Neuro Hand System is an advanced electrotherapy device from Pain Management Technologies (PMT) designed specifically to help relieve pain associated with peripheral neuropathy in the hands and feet.

Key points to know:

• Purpose: It provides a non-invasive, non-narcotic treatment intended to reduce pain, discomfort, and symptoms linked to early onset neuropathy of the hands and feet.

• How it works: 
  - The Ultima Neuro works in tandem with high-quality silver conductive materials (such as conductive therapy gloves and socks) and a grounding cuff.
  - It delivers nerve-targeted electrotherapy signals using refined TENS and micro-current–based settings.
  - The Neuro A and Neuro B modes are the primary neuropathy treatment modes, specifically designed for this type of nerve pain.
  - It also includes TENS sub-modes that can be used to treat a wide range of other nerve pain conditions on different body areas when used with pre-gelled electrode pads.

• Technology & waveform:
  - Built on TENS and micro-current therapy principles, but with narrowed and more precise output parameters focused on peripheral neuropathy in hands and feet.
  - Features an auto-feedback loop that regulates voltage based on tissue mass (for example, adjusting for a heavier vs. thinner person). This helps match treatment to the individual’s tissue impedance.
  - Includes a monophasic waveform typically associated with moving and pushing fluids, which is described as particularly effective for neuropathic pain-related discomfort.

• Treatment approach:
  - Designed to provide circumferential electrotherapy stimulation through wraps (e.g., gloves, socks) that surround the extremity.
  - Can also be used with standard pre-gelled electrodes to treat other parts of the body if needed.
  - Intended as a drug-free, non-surgical option for managing early onset neuropathy symptoms.

• Accessories & configuration:
  - The Ultima Neuro Hand System works together with silver fiber conductive therapy socks and gloves, plus an electrotherapy cuff as the dispersive ground pad.
  - Items such as conductive sock electrodes and grounding cuffs are sold separately.

• Power & design:
  - Uses a slim, long-life Li-ion battery, designed for comfort and convenience during use, including nighttime use when neuropathic pain is often worse and can disturb sleep.

• Product info:
  - Product name: Ultima Neuro Hand System
  - Product code: UNEURO-G
  - Available configurations (depending on kit): device only; device + glove + cuff + spray; or device + sock + cuff + spray.

For more details, kit contents, and any usage instructions or precautions, you can visit the official PMT website at https://paintechnology.com and search for the “Ultima Neuro Hand System,” or check your device manual and Quick Start materials that come with the unit.

Peripheral nerves come out of the spinal cord and are arranged along lines in the body called dermatomes. Usually, damage to a nerve will affect one or more dermatomes, which can be tracked to niche areas of the body. Damage to these nerves disrupts communication between the brain and other parts of the body and can impair muscle movement, prevent normal sensation in the arms/hands and legs/feet, and cause pain.\n\nThere are several different kinds of peripheral neuropathy with a multitude of different causes. They range from Carpal Tunnel Syndrome to Diabetes\n\nSymptoms:\n\nSymptoms are specific to the type of afflicted nerve and may be seen over a period of days, weeks, or years. Electrotherapy probe PR-06A is a Urinary Incontinence Aid Uro Probe designed specifically for treating urinary incontinence and related urinary problems.

• Product name: Electrotherapy probe PR-06A – Urinary Incontinence Aid Uro Probe  
• Product code: PR06A  
• Category: Electrotherapy Supplies – Probes and clips  
• Model: Vaginal Small  
• Product weight: 1 lb  

Key characteristics:
• Specially designed for treating incontinence.  
• Completely fits the body curve and fully adheres to the treatment region to ease muscle contraction.  
• Effectively equipped for use with various incontinence and pelvic muscle stimulators.  
• High safety and steadiness with no over‑stimulation on the treatment region.  

Usage requirements:
• Must be used together with an EMS device to attain desired results.  
• Intended to be used with incontinence stimulators, womancare devices, or other pelvic muscle stimulators whose output is less than 0.25 W/cm².

Indications:
• Urinary incontinence  
• Urinary problems

Actual Answer

Studies have shown that moist heat has greater benefits than dry heat. The heat from ThermoRelief's moist heat pads helps moisture get through the skin barrier and deeper into the tissue beneath. This helps to increase circulation to problem areas, bringing in new blood cells while taking away wastes to speed up recovery.

NO WATER OR SOAKING NEEDED: How can a moist heating pad be effective if you aren't adding moisture? When heated, the soft flannel covering of this hot pad draws in moisture and humidity from the air and retains it, then transfers it to your skin through a process called fomentation. This way, your clothes and skin don't become saturated due to water being held against them.

CONTROLS ARE IN YOUR HANDS: Your skin, problem areas, and heat tolerance are individual to you, so it makes sense to have an adjustable control so that you can customize your care. The state-of-the-art digital controller on this heating pad allows you to adjust the temperature to 113, 130, 150, or 167 degrees and set auto shutoff time control at 15, 30, 45, and 60-minute intervals, all depending on your needs.

PRODUCT CODE: S766-DC

King Weighted - 26 by 14
King - 26 by 14
Medium- 18 x 14

DESCRIPTION
Get relief, heal faster, and get your life back without having to spend tons of money on expensive treatments. The soothing moist heat of this moist heat heating pad is most commonly recommended and used by medical professionals such as doctors, chiropractors, and physical therapists.

Actual Answer

NerveSpa Shoulder Pro is a joint & mobility electrotherapy system from Pain Management Technologies designed specifically for shoulder-related pain and joint support.

Key points you should know:

• Product name & code:
  - NerveSpa Shoulder Pro Joint & Mobility System – Product Code: NSSHLD90

• Primary purpose:
  - A noninvasive treatment system developed for provider-guided care in shoulder pain, joint deterioration, and mobility-related pathways.
  - Designed to help reduce pain and other symptoms associated with:
    • Osteoarthritis of the shoulder (OA)
    • Rheumatoid arthritis of the shoulder (RA)
    • Tendonitis and bursitis
    • Degenerative shoulder conditions
    • General shoulder pain and function limitations

• What it is designed to do:
  - Relieve Pain: Helps reduce pain and other symptoms around the shoulder joint.
  - Improve Function: Supports range of motion, activity tolerance, and day-to-day shoulder function with repeated use.
  - Support Joint Health: Designed to help maintain overall shoulder joint health and support progressive joint-focused care.
  - Promote Circulation: Targets the shoulder region to support localized circulation and therapeutic tissue engagement.

• Programming & technology:
  - Hybrid Programming: Combines NerveSpa and Jstim-derived joint stimulation concepts in one system for joint-focused care.
  - Dual-program structure:
    • Mode 1: Monophasic spike wave at 7.83 Hz (NerveSpa programming)
    • Mode 2: Monophasic pulse / high-volt galvanic wave (Jstim programming)
  - This hybrid architecture is designed to support comfort, circulation, overall joint health, and deeper joint-focused treatment pathways.

• Wearable, joint-focused design:
  - Wearable shoulder format that surrounds the shoulder joint region.
  - Delivers therapy across the joint and adjacent support structures, including tissue, tendons, ligaments, cartilage, and surrounding anatomical zones involved in joint stability, deterioration resistance, and mobility.
  - Intended for repeated one-hour therapy sessions.

• Clinical use context:
  - Built for provider-guided shoulder pain, joint health, and mobility workflows.
  - Clinical pathways where it may be used include:
    • Shoulder arthritis support (OA/RA) – pain relief and symptom reduction while helping maintain function and comfort.
    • Tendonitis and bursitis support – for tendon and bursa irritation or repetitive-use inflammation as part of broader care plans.
    • Recovery and function-oriented use – within recovery programs focused on mobility, function, and tolerance.
    • Joint preservation positioning – support for long-term shoulder joint health and continuity between in-clinic visits.
  - Positioned as a non-drug, noninvasive option within broader provider-guided care.

• Home-use continuity & supplies:
  - Provider-Guided Continuity: Designed to extend in-clinic treatment into the home.
  - Standard kit supply configuration:
    • Includes bicep pads and shoulder pads so patients can follow structured home-use protocols.
    • Includes 3 packs of bicep pads and 3 packs of shoulder pads.
  - Extension strap options are available to support broader fit and longer-term joint care continuity.

• Fit and sizing:
  - Fits small to X-large shoulders in standard configuration.
  - Extender straps are needed for XXL–XXXL sizing.

• Quick specs snapshot:
  - Product Code: NSSHLD90
  - Primary Use: Shoulder joint treatment support
  - Programming: NerveSpa + Jstim hybrid
  - Fit: Small to X-large (extenders for larger sizes)

• Category placement:
  - Listed under Electrotherapy Devices within the NerveSpa family of joint and neuropathy-focused systems.

For more details, purchasing information, or to see how NerveSpa Shoulder Pro fits into your specific care plan, you can visit the official PMT website: https://paintechnology.com or review the Electrotherapy Devices section and NerveSpa Shoulder Pro product page there. Always follow your healthcare provider’s guidance when integrating this system into a treatment plan.

Actual Answer

Neck Traction with Air Pump Technology by Theratrac Air - Cervical Traction Device for Pain Relief, Cervicalgia, Disc Degeneration, Spondylosis, and Spine Alignment

Experience effective neck pain relief with the Theratrac Air Cervical Traction Device.

This innovative device gently pulls the head away from the neck to reduce compression and alleviate pressure on the spine. It is ideal for conditions such as cervicalgia, disc degeneration, spondylosis, and for improving spine alignment. The Theratrac Air provides a light, comfortable stretch to promote recovery and pain relief.

Key features:
• User-Friendly Design: Operate this cervical traction device easily with the included handheld Air Pump.
• Adjustable Neck Support: Customize the width of the neck support using the adjustable knobs to fit all neck sizes comfortably.
• Variable Intensity: Control the stretch intensity with an easy-to-use pump, ensuring a gentle and effective stretch every time.
• Professional Quality: Get clinical-grade cervical traction at home, reducing the need for frequent chiropractor visits.
• Portable Therapy Solution: Lightweight and portable, the Theratrac Air allows you to continue your therapy sessions anywhere you go.

Product code: TTGAIR

Actual Answer

Rapid Knee L1832 (wrap-on knee wrap)

Portable, comfortable and convenient to use. Made from breathable, non-allergenic, and lightweight materials. Provides control and support through an adjustable ROM (range of motion) hinge.

Key features:
- Easy to use and easy to read indexed hinge that indicates ROM improvement
- Hinge allows dynamic or static setting
- Polycentric adjustable design to control extension and flexion

Product codes / sizes (examples from catalog):
- RK100-M – Medium
- RK100-L – Large
- RK100-XL – XL
- RK100-XXL – 2XL
- RK100-3XL – 3XL
(Additional sizes such as Small are also listed in the size range.)

Description:
The Rapid Knee utilizes an adjustable ROM hinge and an open wrap configuration to provide convenience and comfort to the user. The wrap fits snugly and provides even compression and support.

The Rapid Knee has double upright ROM hinges and a locking joint for positioning the knee. This joint can lock the knee into either a flexion or extension position. The adjustable flexion and extension joint enables the practitioner to set limits on flexion and extension but allows the patient to move the knee freely within those limits.

The Rapid Knee is designed for a patient who can load weight on the knee and is capable of some ambulation. It is typically used for post-surgical rehabilitation following knee surgery.

Operation (overview):
The Rapid Knee is operated by first sizing the patient to the appropriate brace, based on knee circumference. While in a sitting position, the Rapid Knee can be unwrapped (outer straps and inner straps opened) for application and adjustment.

Actual Answer

The Polar Vortex is a cold water therapy machine (not a TENS unit) designed to deliver therapeutic cold using continuously circulating water through a universal therapy pad.

Key points from PMT’s information:

• Product name: Polar Vortex – Cold Water Therapy Device with Universal Pad for Treatment of Disability  
• Product code: PV105

• Therapeutic cold water: Uses a continuous flow of cold water to help reduce swelling and inflammation for individuals with a disability.

• Portable and quiet: Compact design for easy portability and storage, with a quiet motor suitable for use at home or in a hospital setting.

• Digital controls: Built-in LCD screen with adjustable settings so you can control treatment time and motor intensity, and monitor water temperature. Controls are easy to read and operate.

• Easy to add ice: Clear, insulated cooler walls let you see the fill line and ice level at a glance, so you know when to refill to keep the cold circulation going.

• Universal therapy pad: Includes a flexible universal pad that can be used on multiple body areas such as the knee, shoulder, elbow, and more. Both the pad and the device feature insulated tubing to help keep the water colder for longer.

• Treatment control: Allows you to set treatment time up to 30 minutes and adjust pressure intensity by liters per minute (LPM). A temperature monitor plus the visible ice level help keep your cryotherapy within the desired range.

• Included components:
  - Universal cold therapy pack x1  
  - Insulated circulating water tube x1  
  - Elastic bandages x3  
  - DC 12V power adapter x1  
  - Product user manual x1

• Specifications:
  - Product weight: 7.89375 lbs  
  - Length: 33 cm  
  - Width: 33 cm  
  - Height: 30 cm

This system provides controlled, portable cold-water therapy using a universal pad; it does not deliver electrical stimulation like a TENS unit.

Actual Answer

Water Circulating Sleep Pad by Thermacycle; Cools and Heats to create a Therapeutic Under Pillow or Fitted Sheet Pad - All Natural Sleep Aid for Hot Flashes, Night Sweat, Menopause, Insomnia, Hyperhidrosis – Pillow Pad

THERMACYCLE MATTRESS PAD: A climate-controlled, heating/cooling mattress pad that will transform your bed into the ideal sleeping environment. The Thermacycle pad fits seamlessly on your current mattress (under the fitted sheet) for an exceptional night’s sleep and year-round comfort. This mattress topper is a solution for a mattress or topper that is too hot or too cold. Sleep comfortably no matter what the climate is with this heating and cooling sleep pad.

SINGLE ZONE HYDRO-BASED HEATING AND COOLING: Thermacycle’s single temperature zone allows you to control the climate of your mattress with the click of a button. Unlike some other heating and cooling pads, this deluxe water-based system doesn’t generate EMF radiation, helping provide a consistent and comfortable sleep environment. It fits single, twin, twin XL and double size beds.

SUPPORTS REM AND RESTORATIVE SLEEP: Thermacycle’s constant climate control technology helps maintain a comfortable temperature so you can experience deeper, more restful sleep. This mattress cooling technology can help reduce hot flashes in menopausal adults and is presented as a leading sleep aid option for adults who struggle with temperature-related sleep disruption.

REDUCES NIGHT SWEATS AND SOOTHES MUSCLES: Using the cool setting, the water circulating sleep pad can help reduce sweating and provide soothing cool comfort to muscles overnight. Cryotherapy-style cooling can help prevent overheating while you sleep so you wake up feeling more refreshed. This water-cooled mattress pad offers a wide temperature range compared to many other cold therapy style products.

PRODUCT CODE & SIZING: Product code: TCSplt. Available configurations include Twin XL (35" by 75"), Queen (60" by 79"), and a small tubeless pillow pad (16" by 24"), as well as a Unit Only option.

DESCRIPTION: Thermacycle is a self-contained water circulating system that utilizes automatic thermoregulation to maintain an adjustable temperature.

OPERATIONAL HIGHLIGHTS:
• System temperature range: approximately 46°F to 118°F
• Micro-tubes carry the water through the bladder, designed to help prevent seam leaks or bursts
• Fill volume: approximately 27 ounces of water

This is a medical grade hot/cold water circulating mattress pad system that provides thermoregulation to help keep your body at a comfortable temperature during sleep.

Actual Answer

Circulating Ice Machine by Blue Cube – for Knee, Elbow, Shoulder, Back Pain, Post-Surgical Swelling, Hospital Use

This is a cold water therapy system (not a TENS unit) designed to provide therapeutic cold and, on some models, air compression.

Key product details (Blue Cube – Cold + optional Air Compression)

• Therapy type: Cold water therapy, with models available as:
  - Cold Only
  - Cold + Air Compression
• Primary uses: Relief from swelling, edema, and inflammation following surgery or injury, including:
  - Knee
  - Shoulder
  - Elbow
  - Back
  - Other areas where the universal pad can be wrapped
• Typical settings/use environment: Post-surgical recovery, sports injuries, hospital or home use.

Main features

• Air Compression (Cold + Air model):
  - Uses continuous flow of cold water combined with air pressure to help with swelling, edema, and inflammation after surgery.

• Cold Water Therapy:
  - Provides a continuous flow of cold water to engulf the treatment area and deliver therapeutic cold therapy.

• Pump Speeds / Temperature (Cold-Only Blue Light model for disability treatment):
  - Two pump speeds:
    - High Flow delivers approximately 47°F
    - Low Speed delivers approximately 52°F

• Portability and Noise Level:
  - Compact, portable ice therapy system
  - Quiet motor for more comfortable and convenient use during recovery.

• Blue Light:
  - Integrated blue light can be turned on or off as needed (for select Blue Light models).

• Digital Controls:
  - Built-in LCD screen
  - Adjustable settings to control:
    - Treatment time (up to 60 minutes)
    - Motor intensity (flow rate in Liters Per Minute – LPM)
  - Temperature monitoring via the display and clear basin
  - Convenient, easy-to-read controls.

• Easy to Add Ice:
  - Clear, insulated cooler walls let you see the fill line and monitor ice levels
  - Visual access helps you know when to refill to maintain effective cooling range.

• Universal Therapy Pad:
  - Included universal pad designed for multiple body areas:
    - Knee
    - Shoulder
    - Elbow
    - Back and other suitable regions
  - Flexible pad with insulated tubing to help keep temperatures cooler for longer periods.

Included components (per PMT listing)

• Blue Cube therapy machine with universal pad x1
• Elastic stretchy bands x2 (for securing the pad to the body)
• DC 12V power adapter x1
• Product user manual x1

Core specifications

• Product code: BCold (for the cold-only model)
• Therapy: Cold only, or cold + air compression (depending on exact model purchased)
• Treatment time: Adjustable, up to 60 minutes
• Flow / compression: Adjustable motor intensity measured in Liters Per Minute (for applicable models)
• Product weight: Approximately 1 lb (for the cold-only model listed)

Notes

• The system is a water-based cold therapy device, not an electrical TENS stimulator.
• For product availability, purchasing options, or model-specific details, you can visit: https://paintechnology.com/category and search for “Circulating Ice Machine by Blue Cube” or product code BCold.

Actual Answer

Splitter Hose by Thermacycle – Connect Pillow Pad and Mattress Pad at the Same Time.

The Splitter Hose by Thermacycle is designed for use with Thermacycle water therapy sleep systems so you can run two pads from a single unit at the same time (for example, a Thermacycle Pillow Pad and a Thermacycle Mattress Pad).

Key things to know:

• Purpose: The splitter hose allows you to connect two compatible Thermacycle pads to one Thermacycle water therapy control unit simultaneously.
• Typical use: Commonly used to run a Thermacycle Mattress Pad (under your fitted sheet) and a Thermacycle Pillow Pad (under your pillow or pillowcase) at the same time, so both your body and head/neck area share the same climate-controlled temperature.
• System type: Thermacycle is a single-zone, hydro-based heating and cooling system. With the splitter, both pads share that single temperature setting from the same control unit.
• Operation: You plug the splitter hose into the outlet on the Thermacycle unit, then plug each pad’s hose into the two ends of the splitter. The unit then circulates temperature-controlled water through both pads together.
• Comfort focus: The Thermacycle system is designed as a climate-controlled mattress/pillow solution to help regulate sleeping temperature for issues such as hot/cold mattresses, hot flashes, night sweats, menopause-related temperature swings, insomnia, or general temperature discomfort at night.
• EMF-related design: The Thermacycle water-based system provides heating and cooling without generating EMF radiation, supporting a consistent and comfortable sleep environment.

If you need details on a specific Thermacycle pad (Pillow Pad vs. Mattress Pad), or help confirming compatibility with your existing Thermacycle unit, you can also check the product pages on https://paintechnology.com or contact PMT customer service for exact model matching.

Actual Answer

Soft-Touch Carbon Electrodes with cloth back (PMT gel) are PMT’s self-adhesive, reusable TENS/EMS electrodes designed for durability, comfort, and wide compatibility.

Key product details (cloth back, PMT gel):

• Product type: Soft-Touch Carbon Electrodes, cloth back (PMT gel)
• Connector: Pig-tail pin connector (standard pin-type)
• Compatibility: Universal for almost all TENS, EMS, Interferential (IF), and Russian stimulation units that use a pin-type connector
• Resistance: 45 ohms of resistance
• Reusability:
  – Reusable, with an average life of about 10–15 uses per pad (or more, depending on use time and care)
  – Self-adhesive, reusable disposable electrode
• Backing material:
  – Cloth back with resilient blue fabric
  – Oval corners to help minimize edge curl
• Gel:
  – High-quality PMT conductive gel
  – Electrode life-span and effectiveness can be enhanced by rubbing a small drop of water on the pad after use
  – Always keep pads in the airtight/resealable pouch directly after use to help preserve the gel

Pack configuration & sizes (cloth back carbon versions):

PMT lists multiple product codes/pack configurations for Soft-Touch Carbon Electrodes with cloth back (PMT gel):

1) PRODUCT CODE: FA3000
   • One dual-wired electrode per package
   • Size: 2.0” x 2.0”
   • Product Weight: 1 lb
   • Width: 5 cm
   • Usage: For use with almost all TENS units and EMS devices, IF, and Russian units
   • Back: Resilient blue fabric

2) PRODUCT CODE: FA3360
   • Self-adhesive, high-quality gel electrodes
   • Reusable (10–15 or more uses per pad)
   • Universal for all TENS and EMS, IF, Russian units with pin-type connector
   • Sizes/quantities:
     – 2.0" x 2.0": 10 packs of 4 electrodes per pack
     – 2.0" Round: 10 packs of 4 electrodes per pack
     – 3" Round: 10 packs of 4 electrodes per pack
     – Butterfly 6" x 3.3": 10 packs of 1 electrode per pack
   • Product Weight: 1 lb
   • Width: 5 cm
   • Back: Resilient blue fabric

3) PRODUCT CODE: FA2000
   • Self-adhesive, high-quality gel electrodes
   • Reusable (10–15 or more uses per pad)
   • 4 electrodes per package
   • Universal for all TENS and EMS, IF, Russian units with pin-type connector
   • Sizes/quantities:
     – 2.0" x 2.0": 10 packs of 4 electrodes per pack
     – 2.0" Round: 10 packs of 4 electrodes per pack
     – 3" Round: 10 packs of 4 electrodes per pack
     – Butterfly 6" x 3.3": 10 packs of 1 electrode per pack
   • Product Weight: 1 lb
   • Width: 5 cm

General Soft-Touch line characteristics (applies to these electrodes):

• Construction: Non-porous tricot or cloth backing, conductive wire-mount layer, high-quality carbon layer, and extra-thick gel
• Connector fit: Ultra-tight pigtail connector with a snug plug
• Shape: Oval corners to reduce edge lift/curl
• Gel quality: Latex-free USA gel, packaged in resealable bags to help keep pads fresh
• Therapy types: Effective for use with TENS, EMS, interferential, microcurrent, and galvanic generators (where specified)

Care & replacement guidance from PMT:

• After each use, you can rub a small drop of water on the pad surface to help extend electrode life and maintain stickiness.
• Always place pads back into the airtight, resealable pouch immediately after use.
• For best therapy results, PMT recommends replacing electrodes once the conductive gel starts to wear down or the pads no longer adhere or feel consistent; this typically occurs after about 10–15 uses per pad, depending on how long each session lasts.

If you tell me which exact size or product code you’re using (FA3000, FA3360, or FA2000), I can narrow the details to that specific configuration.
